- **agent-hooks**: PreToolUse, SessionEnd, SessionStart, Stop, SubagentStop, UserPromptExpansion, UserPromptSubmit on Bash, Edit|Write. Fixers before each write and Stop lanes on the session scope, a subagent's at its own SubagentStop, in every repository that holds .claude/agent-hooks.jsonc, the labels a human's own slash command puts on, and on Bash in every repository a landing guard and a per-task worktree guard ([bengous/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/bengous/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/agent-hooks/hooks/hooks.json) · ★6)
- **agent-hooks**: PreCompact, SessionStart, Stop ([danieliser/agent-hooks](https://github.com/danieliser/agent-hooks/blob/HEAD/hooks/hooks.json) · ★1)
- **agent-hooks**: PreToolUse, UserPromptSubmit on Bash|PowerShell. Refuse inline interpreter bodies in Bash and PowerShell tool calls, refuse a directory change combined with other commands, refuse an in-place sed edit in text mode on Windows, and warn as session context usage crosses 50% and each 10% band above. ([astenlund/agent-hooks](https://github.com/astenlund/agent-hooks/blob/HEAD/hooks/hooks.json))
- **agent-hooks**: PreToolUse on Bash|Write|Edit|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit|mcp__.*. AtlaSent guard: destructive and shipping actions wait for a person ([AtlaSent/atlasent-mcp-server](https://github.com/AtlaSent/atlasent-mcp-server/blob/HEAD/packages/agent-hooks/hooks/hooks.json))
- **agent-hooks**: PreToolUse on Write|Edit|MultiEdit|Bash|REPL. Ask CodeRifts before Write/Edit of a contract artifact; deny a Bash command that names one with a write shape; match REPL and read tool_input.code as text. Host timeout is 8s (seconds). Gate budget is 5000ms. The PreToolUse command sets "onFailure": "block", so a host timeout blocks the tool call. ([coderifts/agent-hooks](https://github.com/coderifts/agent-hooks/blob/HEAD/hooks/hooks.json))
